```text
Step 5: add the category counts: 4 + 3 = 7.
```

## 18. sb_rowT_locate_515faac20939a5126d333a0d

Question: How many objects are there altogether among the chair(s) and monitor(s) in this room?

```text
<think>

Step 1: list distinct instances of chair: tag_a.

Locate: monitor instance tag_a in frame 4.

Step 3: list distinct instances of monitor: tag_a; tag_b; tag_c; tag_d.
Step 4: So the count is 4.
Step 5: add the category counts: 1 + 4 = 5.
</think>
```

## 26. sb_rowT_locate_8ef33a8c4c5b25c45356f4d7

Question: Count the cabinet(s) and door(s) and heater(s) in this room together. How many are there in total?

```text
<think>
Locate: cabinet instance tag_a in frame 1 (middle).
Locate: cabinet instance tag_b in frame 3 (left).
Step 1: list distinct instances of cabinet: tag_a; tag_b.
Step 2: So the count is 2.
Locate: door instance tag_a in frame 7 (right).
Step 3: list distinct instances of door: tag_a.
Step 4: So the count is 1.
Locate: heater instance tag_a in frame 1 (middle).
Step 5: list distinct instances of heater: tag_a.
Step 6: So the count is 1.
Step 7: add the category counts: 2 + 1 = 3; 3 + 1 = 4.
</think>

4
```

## 30. sb_rowT_locate_a1a88fe9b8e0a6768aeb18c6

Question: How many objects are there altogether among the keyboard(s) and table(s) in this room?

```text
<think>

Locate: keyboard instance tag_b in frame 6.
Locate: keyboard instance tag_c in frame 8.

Step 2: So the count is 3.
Locate: table instance tag_a in frame 1.

Locate: table instance tag_c in frame 4.
Step 3: list distinct instances of table: tag_a; tag_b; tag_c.
Step 4: So the count is 3.
Step 5: add the category counts: 3 + 3 = 6.
</think>

6
```
